6. Missing Entware stops dependent install/setup/startup and explains the amtm installation/mount requirement. Preserve settings and saved activation intent. Missing Scribe alone leaves optional custom logging unavailable without blocking otherwise ready publishing. CFMgr installs its selected packages, never Entware itself, and never repartitions or formats storage.
7. JFFS boot hooks register saved eligible cron entries regardless of mount; each fired handler checks its own prerequisites. Dependent work defers promptly until all required gates pass, with no boot sleep/polling loop or substitute storage. Matching post-mount/later ticks retry eligible deferred scope under bounded admission/backoff.
8. Backup/restore check the chosen mounted backup drive independently; it need not be the Entware drive. Validate the selected operation's tool prerequisites and any required tunnel payload storage. Native recovery remains available without Entware, but does not imply the full backup/restore workflow works without its selected tools. Core recovery/update/setup transactions do not require a backup drive.
9. Keep saved Off, installation/removal guards and quarantine distinct from storage/network waiting. Eligible cron never bypasses execution gates, erases preferences or starts unrelated services.

Keep native recovery/status and safe Off/removal available without working Entware. Missing shared prerequisites block operational work under R36/D55 without resetting configuration or saved activation intent; scoped tunnel/logging dependencies gate only their own work. Core rollback copies are small/bounded JFFS files, temporary work and DDNS/IP-Sync observations are RAM, and large tunnel data/logs remain Entware. Backup destination setup is optional; frequent state writes never become frequent JFFS writes.

## Configuration, menus, and feature setup

### Base installation and initial state

A genuinely fresh install writes the JFFS entry point/small bundled helpers and initializes `/jffs/addons/CFMgr.d/config` with schema/basic defaults and blank provider settings. First inspect retained identity/config/data and unresolved guards before defaults: first installation after completed retained-config uninstall reconstructs valid saved setup using current code, as specified below. Corrupted/missing required retained data requires quarantine/repair. Fresh features start unconfigured/off; base installation checks the shared prerequisites while native recovery diagnostics need no Entware. No provider prompts or remote creation in base installation/reconstruction.

| Initial setting | Value / behavior |
| --- | --- |
| DDNS | Configured: false; enabled: false. |
| Cloudflared | Configured: false; enabled: false; installed: false; maintenance_enabled: false; no binary/tunnel installation. |
| IP-Sync | Configured: false; enabled: false; no list updates or retry job. |
| Feature logging/rotation | All three custom logfile integrations inactive; no Scribe detection, log directory, or rotation rules. Default policy values may be stored but are unused until that feature's setup. |
| Backup | Destination unset; no `CFBackup` creation or backup until the user sets it up. |
| Developer mode | `developer=false` in main config; editable manually only, no menu toggle. |
| Existing installation | Live `u`/`uf` preserves/migrates settings and stays manager-only. Completed retained removal invokes first-install reconstruction; interrupted removal cannot auto-rebuild. Legacy services remain untouched until reviewed migration. |

Initial custom-logging-off placeholders are not explicit user opt-outs for configured features. First dedicated setup can auto-enable that feature's logging policy when Scribe is ready; after setup, preserve saved preferences/off choices on repeat setup, launch, repair, and restore. Native system-message diagnostics are independent of these custom logfile preferences.

### Main menu and submenus

Use firmware-native shell menus with mixed numeric/text choices and focused nesting, following Skynet's main-menu-to-`Menu_*` structure. Each main action owns its setup/settings/operations; deeper settings can have their own submenu. Do not collect all features' options into one wizard or expose unrelated settings in a feature menu. Parse exact complete choice tokens so `uf` cannot dispatch as `u`; unknown input is rejected without an action.

| Main choice | Dedicated submenu options | Availability / isolation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0) Check status` | Print the current setup summary and enabled-feature details, then return to the main menu. | First choice at the top; read-only and available before any feature setup. Apply the status contract below. |
| `1) DDNS` | Setup/import or edit credentials/zone/records; On/Off; update now/status; link to shared address-family settings; IPv6/TTL/proxy/timeout/schedule; logging submenu Re-detect Scribe, Install/enable logging, Uninstall logging; owned integration re-detect/repair. | Off removes owned operational hooks/jobs while keeping configured state, keys, zone/record settings, logging preference/files/rules and independent Scribe rotation. On reinstalls/repairs operational integration. Fixed logging policy; no tunnel/IP-Sync setup. |
| `2) Cloudflared` | A token or B advanced setup (`Auto-detect/import? [y/N]`); maintenance submenu Install/reinstall binary/updater/watchdog, Uninstall binary/updater/watchdog and binary-update settings; tunnel On/Off (Start/Stop aliases), Restart/status; edit token/method; Re-detect config; readiness/watchdog; logging submenu Re-detect Scribe, Install/enable logging, Uninstall logging and rotation tuning. | One tunnel/router; existing A/B/maintenance contracts retained. On recovers missing binary for valid saved tunnel, no creation; Off clears tunnel+maintenance activation/runtime hooks/jobs but preserves logging prefs/logs/rules and independent Scribe rotation. Restart requires On. Logging uninstall separately handles absent Scribe/current writer without enabling Off. |
| `3) IP-Sync` | Guided account/token setup/import; fetch primary/second IP lists, select number (at most 10 lists) or exact case-sensitive name, automatically save returned name/ID; On/Off; publish now/status; explicit provider-read-only target check (legacy dry-run equivalent); link to shared address-family settings; queue/retry; logging submenu Re-detect Scribe, Install/enable logging, Uninstall logging; owned integration re-detect/repair. | Off removes own operational hooks/jobs, retains configured state/token/account/list settings/log preferences/files/rules and independent Scribe rotation. On repairs operational integration. Explicit target check may read configured Off state without publishing/enabling; normal cooldown/auth containment applies and ordinary status remains local. No DDNS/Cloudflared dependency; fixed policy. |
| `4) Backup / Restore` | Select mounted destination/date format/count; create manual backup; numbered archive list/inspect/verify; review/confirm restore; delete a selected archive. | Manages existing configured features and manager config; setup of this menu does not activate a feature. Retain established `CFBackup` naming/3–5 retention rules. |
| `fx) Fix invalid credentials` (conditional) | Open the same serialized credential-only repair flow for persisted local auth faults; show redacted affected feature/credential labels. | Present only when a repairable auth fault exists; no full wizard, remote identity replacement or rate-limit token prompt. Cancel leaves affected Off/fault saved; intentional Off/shared unrelated work preserved. |
| `ao) Advanced options` | Shared address-family settings; manager channel/version/configuration/install inspection; Reinstall Entware dependencies; configured integration repair; full manager uninstall; Initialise / reset everything. | Dependency reinstall handles CFMgr's selected Entware packages only under R36/D55; it does not reinstall Cloudflared daemon/hooks/workers or alter feature activation. Uninstall reviews scope then exact `Wipe everything? [y/N]` (default retain). Reset confirms owned wipe while retaining manager. No developer/menu auto-update toggle or global Scribe action. |
| `u) Check for CFMgr updates` | Unless developer=true, bounded release/version check; newer compatible package shows versions and asks `Update CFMgr? [y/n]`. | Developer mode reports check/offer suppression without lookup. Otherwise decline/current/error preserves installation. Cloudflared updates remain independent. |
| `uf) Force CFMgr update / reinstall` | Explicitly fetch/reinstall selected manager package even current, with full manifest/hash/version/schema validation. | Manager-only; no downgrade/reset. With developer=true require clear confirmation that local developer code will be replaced. Force bypasses current skip only, never validation or preservation. |
| `e) Exit` | Leave manager; native setup e saves draft progress and exits Setup. | Normal submenus use Back. DDNS/IP-Sync keep completed private draft fields on e/Ctrl+C and resume later, never activate incomplete setup. Cloudflared allows e before reviewed Save; throughout mutation e is refused, Ctrl+C performs bounded cleanup and preserves recovery state. Normal navigation returns after verified completion/rollback or guarded failure. |

Show concise configured/enabled labels for three features. Selecting a feature opens its submenu; Setup handles first setup/reviewed reconfiguration. On/Off preserves saved inputs; Cloudflared import/re-detect is B-only, A is token entry; runtime/tuning requires valid completed setup. Entering/backing out never enables. Each configured feature owns Scribe re-detect and separate Install/enable logging / Uninstall logging, including while service Off. Re-detect never overrides logging opt-out; explicit reviewed enable required. No separate runtime Stop logging/Stop rotation toggle, global Scribe action or native-feature rotation tuning. Service On/Off never starts/stops/restarts Scribe or changes its rotation schedule/global policy. Apply shared cancellation/recovery to actions.

The Skynet reference is captured `IPSet_ASUS/firewall.sh`: `Load_Menu` dispatches choices, `Menu_Settings` nests categories, and `Show_Menu`/`Return_To_Menu` handle display/navigation. CFMgr uses those organizational patterns with the passive base-install behavior defined here.

### Check status

`cfmgr --diagnostic` and `cfmgr --doctor` are identical standalone health-check aliases, extending choice 0's quick local status. Reuse its safe read-only feature summary when available, then add platform/dependency/command checks. Broken config or dependencies mark the affected summary unavailable without stopping independent diagnostic checks. Both aliases bypass operational startup, package installation and automatic repair. Report non-secret model (where available), firmware components, kernel release/architecture, known userspace ABI and command versions; unknown context is labelled unknown, never guessed from the kernel. Do not dump NVRAM, environment, config, IP/MAC/serial/hostname, tokens or mount labels.

Use stable check IDs with PASS/FAIL/SKIP, a concise reason and the evidence level (presence, option parsing or functional synthetic probe). Report exact missing dependency/package/capability names. An unavailable/unverified Entware mount makes its package state unavailable; it is not proof the packages are absent. Separate missing commands, installed-but-unusable/wrong-ABI/library failures, unsupported arguments and downstream skips with the failed prerequisite ID. Include the actual flags/capability tested so developers can propose narrower follow-up checks. Optional unsupported commands such as command -v must not falsely imply normal CFMgr cannot run when its selected lookup path works.

The check does not start/stop services, reinstall packages, execute provider/callback actions or access Cloudflare. It may create only bounded synthetic files/locks/archives inside its newly created private temporary directory and must clean up owned probes on completion/signals. No repair prompt is implicit in a report. Normal report output contains fixed/sanitized diagnostics, not raw stderr or private input. Keep package/runtime command inventory aligned with implemented code as features are added. A first native-only report may explicitly defer Entware execution until mount/tool verification exists; it does not complete R37/V75. Without a verified supervisor, finite native probes do not establish a hard deadline for a hung system executable; do not claim otherwise.

`0) Check status` reports basic manager version/channel and storage availability, then the three feature states. Use literal `Configured: true/false` and `Enabled: true/false` flags. Only configured, enabled features receive detailed output; configured does not mean enabled, running, or healthy.

| Saved state | Output / work allowed |
| --- | --- |
| No features configured | Print the basic summary and each feature as `Disabled`, `Configured: false`, `Enabled: false`; say `Configure at least one feature first: DDNS, Cloudflared, or IP-Sync.` No provider/service probes. |
| Feature disabled, whether configured or not | Print Disabled/configured/enabled flags only, with no feature probes/settings/runtime/log details. Separate redacted setup-incomplete/resume notice or auth-fault/repair notice and fx may appear without credential details. A saved draft is not configured or enabled. |
| Feature configured and enabled | Print flags and relevant details below. Externally stopped/failed/deferred process remains saved On; show observed state accurately. CFMgr Stop is Off. |
| Configured features exist but all are disabled | Show the disabled summaries and explain that a configured feature can be enabled from its submenu; do not tell the user to repeat setup. |
| Invalid state: enabled without configured | Report invalid configuration/needs setup without probing, silently changing flags, or presenting a working service. Status is read-only; the separate manager-startup integrity guard contains affected work and offers reviewed repair. |

| Enabled feature | Status information |
| --- | --- |
| DDNS | Selected logical A/AAAA scope, allowed families and per-family current source state/age. Show confirmed updated/removed/unchanged and partial/deferred outcomes plus current-state cleanup pending. IPv6 testing label stays provisional; up to five bounded success entries distinguish address publication from removal, no status probes. |
| Cloudflared | Installed binary version; last successful binary update time (initial install is separate); owned connector process state/uptime, stopped/deferred reason, and separately observed tunnel connectivity. Show custom logfile location, Scribe/logrotate readiness, saved versus installed rule status, size/count/time/compression policy, and Scribe check cadence when custom logging is active. |
| IP-Sync | Primary/secondary mapping, current per-family active/inactive/unknown state and age, last verified remote equality or pending drift/cleanup. Provisional IPv6 label; empty-list clearing is not list deletion. Up to five bounded outcome/history entries name target and add/remove/unchanged result, with no API status calls. |

Status uses bounded local reads and cached/recorded observations: no Cloudflare calls, downloads, sync, service restart, rule repair, forced rotation, or full logrotate dry-run. A status action does not trigger maintenance; normal manager-launch repair remains governed by its separate fast-check contract. Read only the CFMgr-owned rule/necessary include signals, not a dump of all Scribe configuration. An off/unavailable custom logging option shows that state without its inactive policy details. Redact secrets and never print token/certificate/credential content.

Cached success/IP, PID or metrics never prove credentials still authorized. Native publishing reads current targets even when local addresses are unchanged, skips only writes when complete remote state matches, and performs no separate auth poll. Revocation is observed on actual reconciliation requests. Explicit setup/On/fx repair may do bounded read-only auth/target validation even unchanged IP. Throttle retry_due and classified outcome may be shown as recorded enabled-feature state; persistent global redacted fault/conditional fx is separate from disabled-feature details.

Keep bounded DDNS/IP-Sync last-success/attempt/result and up to five recent successful observations under private `/tmp/cfmgr/` RAM state tied to the feature target/generation, independent of Entware/Scribe. They are boot-local, not hidden history logs or frequent JFFS writes; after reboot report Unknown unless a bounded native-log read supplies evidence. Cloudflared install/binary-activation observations can persist under mounted `/opt/var/lib/cfmgr/`. Metadata write failure never changes DDNS completion/IP-Sync API success. Do not infer success from file dates, attempts or version; use Never/Unknown/Unavailable accurately. Partial DDNS failure is not full success; IP-Sync unchanged/readback differs from new publication. Optional archive restored timestamps remain historical observations, not current health.

Prefer each feature's eligible owned logfile/history for recent successful IPs; bound lines/bytes and files read, with a proposed display limit of five. If custom logging is unavailable, a bounded read of accessible native system messages may supply the same tagged DDNS/IP-Sync events without requiring Scribe. Verify the active firmware log location/rotation path instead of assuming a Scribe relocation; filter by feature, target, success marker, and timestamps, deduplicate mirrored copies, and omit credentials/unrelated messages. If history is absent, rotated away, remote-only, or inaccessible, report that clearly; never create a hidden history file or scan indefinitely. Exact native-log parsing/uptime capabilities remain P1/P8 evidence.

### Configuration contract

Use main private config for native API records/A token/full B AccountTag/TunnelSecret/TunnelID/expected identity/typed refs; JSON/token handoff derives from them. Selected cert metadata references both the live Entware file and a validated original-byte JFFS recovery copy with account, supported format, provenance, hash, and generation. This small certificate copy is recovery data, not editable authority: master config owns credentials; the user owns remaining live YAML fields. Capture cert only on successful reviewed login/import/repair replacement, or seed a missing copy once from a trusted account/provenance-validated runtime cert during reviewed schema migration/reinstall/update; never adopt arbitrary edited/foreign certs. Do not duplicate full user YAML in JFFS; KEEP preserves its Entware primary. Default B uses login certificate, not requested management API key; retain optional API credential only if genuinely selected alternative. Safe data parsing/no eval/secret arguments; native schema/format/size proof Pending.

| Group | Settings to support |
| --- | --- |
| General | Data/schema versions, separate configured/enabled states, committed generation, independently readable recovery/install/uninstall/reset guard and retained identity, channel/version, WAN unit/Entware paths/readiness. `developer=false` by default, manual-file-only; suppress manager checks/offers/external callbacks when true. Fresh activation flags false; corrupt or invalid retained config never becomes fresh defaults automatically. |
| Native setup drafts | Separate per-feature candidate inputs/typed credentials, account/zone/name/ID/family preferences, validation state, draft/base revisions and next missing step. No completed configured/enabled claim or live shared-ref mutation. Persist accepted complete fields only; retry/resume through validation and final reviewed promotion. Checkpoints preserve unrelated active settings and other drafts under a short revision-checked lock. |
| Public addresses (shared core) | One selected-WAN helper/dispatcher with independent IPv4 NAT-aware and IPv6 global-host source/egress policies, native candidates, bounded ordered per-family HTTPS endpoints, time/size limits and freshness/debounce settings. Per-feature allowed families/Auto defaults; observations/generations/availability stay RAM. Review legacy family/source preferences at Save, no copied resolver or DDNS-credential dependency. |
| DDNS | Configured logical zone/name/A-AAAA definitions and creation attributes independent of cached provider IDs; typed private credential, TTL/proxy/family preferences and resolver/schedule. Fresh complete remote comparison before no-op; delete confirmed inactive managed records and recreate configured definitions on return with new IDs. No /opt dependency. |
| Tunnel | Selected method/full A token or B AccountTag/TunnelSecret/TunnelID/captured metadata/expected identity/ref/path. Track live Entware cert and validated original-byte JFFS cert copy with account/format/provenance/hash/generation. CLI login/create default; optional management API record only genuinely selected alternative. Trio/runtime generation/requested On/readiness; user owns other YAML/comments, native parser/JSON/login/cert bounds proof Pending. |
| Updates / installation | Cloudflared `installed` observed verified installation, independent `maintenance_enabled` activation, owned/adopted-and-recorded path/digest/provenance, architecture/ABI/tag/asset, saved automatic-binary-update preference/channel/schedule and rollback limits. Manager trusted source/channel/release-commit/manifest/version/schema/helper inventory; stable `main` default, `develop` explicitly selected only. Developer flag changes neither branch nor Cloudflared updater. No durable manual-stop flag or manager startup/cron check. |
| IP-Sync (Access lists) | Account/typed credential, primary list_name/list_id, optional list_name2/list_id2, family mapping and incomplete second-list draft. Each target equals current active router families, including empty/clear intent. RAM cache/queue never supersedes current source/config. No retained-sibling/split-cleanup mode; guided setup fetches IDs, accepted wizard fields win over concurrent manual edits. |
| Provider outcomes / credential repair | Durable non-secret auth_fault/reason/typed credential reference, affected verified dependency scope and prior/requested On repair intent distinct from intentional user Off. Boot-local scoped retry_due/server-delay/attempt/last-confirmed desired state, no frequent JFFS writes. No runtime guards/queues/cooldowns restored as live state; archived fault/intent only advisory. Explicit shared refs, not string-equality scope inference; manual config edits reconcile through validation. |
| Logging | Separate custom-logging preference/readiness, explicit opt-out and bounded owned cleanup metadata per feature. Cloudflared tuning versus fixed native policies unchanged. First fresh ready setup initializes policy; service Off preserves preference/logs/rules and independent Scribe rotation. Uninstall logging saves inactive preference/suppresses writers+self-heal before deletion; explicit Install/enable required after opt-out. Native messages independent; daemon stream not mirrored. |
| Backup | Optional selected mount identity/path, fixed root folder `CFBackup`, `backup_keep` (3–5; proposed default 5), `backup_date_format` (router format where usable, otherwise chosen date order), archive format/version, free-space checks, and eligible owned feature log/history inclusion when Scribe is ready. No backup schedule or daily/weekly/monthly retention tiers. |
| Integration | Owned hook blocks/jobs, prior DDNS settings when changed, install/schema versions, migration provenance. |

Imported values stay private. Public examples use empty/placeholding values. Validate settings and write atomically before activation. Preserve unknown imported tunnel options where possible; do not rebuild an existing YAML file from a narrow template that silently drops them.

`configured` means completed validated setup; enabled is saved On/Off. Missing authority/nonowned required data is invalid; missing derived JSON/owned YAML key with trusted authority and valid user YAML is repairable drift, missing intentionally uninstalled binary installation state. Installed never implies activation. Guard precedes mutations; incomplete generation cannot run. Auth-Off preserves completed local setup with durable fault/requested intent, throttle leaves On waiting. Intentional user Off supersedes repair-On intent; KEEP uninstall separately retains saved intent under installation guard. Data backups never import live execution guards.

### Feature-specific inputs

| Selected action | Minimum user-supplied information | Credentials and lookup rules |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Cloudflare DDNS | Scoped DNS token, zone ID or selectable zone, record name(s); use the shared Auto available-family defaults. | DNS edit/read as needed; zone enumeration may require additional read scope. Accept a supplied zone ID when enumeration is unavailable. Review shared source settings only when changing/importing them; no ISP-specific CGNAT input. |
| Cloudflared maintenance-only install/reinstall | No Cloudflare credentials; mounted writable Entware and reviewed automatic-binary-update preference. | Force-download verified official binary and prepare owned updater/watchdog; maintenance_enabled=true without changing tunnel configured/enabled. No runtime watchdog/start until configured+enabled, no Scribe/provider setup. |
| Cloudflared A — token setup (default) | Connector token copied from the user's dashboard-created/existing tunnel. | Force-download verified direct-release binary and prepare maintenance, save opaque token privately. No account ID/zone/certificate/management API token; routes stay dashboard-managed. |
| Cloudflared B — import y | Completed YAML, matching referenced `<TunnelID>.json`, and any certificate/other files actually required by that config. | Prepare/show private import location, prompt the user to place files, then continue detection/validation/review/Save/activation. Import is advanced-only; preserve application settings and create no replacement remote tunnel. |
| Cloudflared B — fresh n/Enter | Minimum tunnel name/intended account context; reuse validated same-account management certificate or browser authorization via supported login if absent, no API-key default prompt. | Review replacement and Save before installer/login/CLI create. Capture complete JSON authority/new UUID/starter coherently, preserve old generation/foreign files and remote old tunnel. Existing runtime token cannot authorize creation; ordinary launch/import/missing cert alone never invokes login. |
| Re-detect config later | Existing advanced YAML and required referenced files; one explicitly selected candidate. | Reuse B import validation/Save gates; never import a standalone token file through this action or switch/start merely because files exist. |
| IP-Sync setup | Account ID and suitable Zero Trust API token, then guided list selection; list ID automatically fetched. Shared Auto available-family defaults. | Show up to 10 eligible lists: if at most 10, number or exact case-sensitive name; if more, preview then exact-name lookup beyond the preview. Spaces/optional quotes handled literally; validate/confirm returned name+ID before Save, same flow for second list. Token scopes must cover actual operations; DDNS never required/enabled. |
| Feature file logging | No extra initial inputs when Scribe/logrotate is ready: apply that feature's defaults. | Cloudflared tuning remains editable later; DDNS/IP-Sync policy is fixed. Import/restore preserves saved preferences/off state. All three can operate without custom logging; native DDNS/IP-Sync and brief manager diagnostics remain available. |
| Backup | Optional manual-backup destination chosen from numbered mounted-drive choices. | No Cloudflare credential is needed to back up local files; backup setup can be skipped and run later for any configured feature. |

CFMgr supports complete initial connector setup: A saves the user's dashboard token; B either imports completed advanced files or creates a named locally managed tunnel and starter files from scratch. Published applications, private-network routes, DNS routing, and access policies remain user-managed. A and B-import require no creation credentials; fresh B asks only the selected creation/authentication method's inputs. These are required planned features, not available runtime commands yet.

### Cloudflared token and advanced setup

Support exactly one managed tunnel/connector per router, with mutually exclusive token and advanced-config runtime methods. Multiple ingress rules/services inside that one advanced tunnel are allowed; multiple tunnel instances/profiles are outside scope. Both methods use the same lifecycle, watchdog, binary updater, logging, and backup contracts.

Both A/B fresh setup and standalone **Install/reinstall binary, updater & watchdog** use one checked direct-release installer at reviewed Save, force-downloading even if a verified owned binary exists. Privately stage/verify first; publish replacement only under the guarded transaction. Normal On reuses a healthy verified binary, downloading only when missing; normal updater skips the same version. Existing config/token/credentials/logging always survive normal binary reinstall. Base installation is passive except valid retained-config reconstruction. No package feed or cloudflared opkg requirement.

| Cloudflared state / explicit action | configured | enabled | installed | maintenance_enabled | Saved auto-update preference / eligible integration |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Fresh never installed | false | false | false | false | Preference may be stored; no feature integrations. |
| Maintenance-only install/reinstall | unchanged (may be false) | unchanged (may be false) | true after verification | true | If preference On, updater hooks/job only. Runtime start/watchdog requires valid configured+enabled. |
| Configured On / Start | true, validated | true | true; reuse healthy binary or checked missing-binary recovery | true | Runtime/start/WAN/mount/service/watchdog blocks/jobs; updater only if preference On. Start follows readiness gates. |
| Explicit Off / Stop | preserved | false | preserved | false | Remove ALL owned Cloudflared runtime and maintenance hooks/jobs. Keep binary/tunnel files/preferences/logs/rules. Preference creates no job. |
| Later explicit maintenance install while Off | preserved | false | true | true | Updater maintenance may activate from preference; no tunnel start/watchdog. Another explicit Off clears both flags again. |
| Binary/updater/watchdog uninstall | preserved | false | false after verified removal | false | Stop verified owned connector; detach all owned integrations; remove owned/adopted recorded binary/helpers/old/staging on mounted Entware. Keep configured data/logging prefs/logs. |
| Restart | must be true/valid | must already be true | must be verified | must be true | Readiness/bounded replacement only; Off remains Off, never silently enabled. |
| External process kill | unchanged | stays true if On | unchanged | unchanged | Watchdog recovers saved On under readiness/outage/guard gates; no durable manual-stop state. |

All eligible integration additionally requires a healthy trusted generation and no affecting recovery/uninstall/reset guard. `installed` is verified installation availability, not configuration or activation; an absent drive reports unavailable/deferred without discarding saved intent. Menu/status/healthy launch/Off reconciliation never downloads or auto-enables; bounded hook repair follows active flags. Prepared watchdog code is permitted for maintenance-only installation, but its runtime schedule is absent until a valid tunnel is On. Force binary replacement may stop/restart only a currently On verified owned connector after candidate verification; Off/unconfigured never starts. Helper-only repair leaves a healthy process alone.

### Checked Cloudflared acquisition and ownership

The common downloader is allowed only for explicit choice-2 A/B setup or maintenance install/reinstall, valid configured On missing-binary recovery, data restore/retained reconstruction, and eligible scheduled binary update. Bind selected release tag/exact supported architecture-ABI asset from official [cloudflare/cloudflared releases](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflared/releases), with HTTPS origin/tag/name/bounded transfer. Stage first; verify official exact-asset digest/SHA256 then ELF ABI/bounded --version before activation. Self-computed hash alone no trust source; missing/ambiguous/wrong digest/HTML/version/ABI fails closed, old binary intact. Protected old copy/reviewed Save replacement only after successful staging; no persistent version pin/blacklist/skip control.

The official 2026.10.0 release reviewed on 2026-10-08 publishes Linux SHA256s including `cloudflared-linux-arm64`; this is evidence, not a hardcoded latest version. GitHub's [release-assets API](https://docs.github.com/en/rest/releases/assets) exposes a `sha256:` digest. Native metadata parsing and SHA256 capability remain P1 proof: use verified firmware sha256sum or native OpenSSL `dgst -sha256` via one strict 64-hex normalizer/helper, under R36/D55, with no router Python installation or integrity waiver. The reference `jffs/scripts/cloudflared-update.sh` uses fixed `/opt/bin/cloudflared`, GitHub releases/latest Linux ARM64, Python metadata parsing, tag/`--version` and `.old`/pending rollback. Preserve recovery mechanics while replacing its Python parsing and adding required digest checks.

Preserve the reference binary updater's explicit downgrade refusal as well as its same-version skip. Compare normalized supported numeric versions before download and again under the activation lock; reject malformed/unsupported version forms. Explicit force-install/reinstall may reinstall the same or a newer supported version, but force never authorizes a downgrade. Restoration of the protected prior binary after a failed activation is the recovery exception, not a release-selection bypass. Freeze comparison/version-floor and idle-versus-On transaction semantics in P1.

| Lookup / ownership boundary | Required behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Bounded lookup | Canonical `/opt/bin/cloudflared` first, then fixed supported standard binary directories and one bounded configured legacy path. Freeze exact platform whitelist in P1 (candidates `/opt/sbin`, `/jffs/bin`, `/usr/local/bin`, `/usr/bin`); no recursive filesystem scan. |
| Candidate identity | Verify regular file/resolved path/mount/permissions, ELF architecture/ABI and bounded cloudflared `--version`; compare known CFMgr digest/provenance when available. Product version does not identify an owning service. Reject unrelated files/wrappers/unverified symlinks/read-only firmware replacement targets. |
| Foreign/shared controller | Inspect recorded init/hooks/package/shared-binary/controller ownership before mutation. Preserve foreign enabled service/connectors/configs and unclaimed binaries; require explicit reviewed migration before replacement/adoption. Never silently disable foreign init or stop by process name alone. |
| Removal | Remove only CFMgr-owned or explicitly adopted-and-recorded verified binary/helper/old/staging paths on the verified mount. Preserve unclaimed copies/foreign configs/hooks; no blanket cloudflared-directory deletion or symlink traversal into foreign roots. |

Historical feed evidence remains D24: recipe/default/sample sources exist, but no cloudflared entry was found in aarch64-k3.10 on 2026-10-08. The selected direct-release route supersedes that initial opkg dependency; foreign package/controller provenance still matters for reviewed migration.

| Setup choice | CFMgr action | Completion / activation gate |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A) Token — default | Show account-neutral dashboard guidance; collect connector token. Review Save/enable, then force-download checked official binary, prepare owned maintenance and save masked input privately. | No file-import/YAML/JSON/certificate needed. Validate capability/token shape before activation; local checks do not prove remote acceptance. |
| B) Advanced config | Ask `Auto-detect/import existing advanced config? [y/N]`. y enters prepare/place/continue/import validation; n or blank enters complete fresh named-tunnel creation. Both include binary/updater/watchdog installation at Save. | Fresh setup warns about existing selected files and replaces them only through the reviewed save transaction. Import requires valid YAML and every required referenced file. Only a validated committed generation may become configured/enabled and start. |
| Re-detect config / import | Advanced-only action, reusing B-y preparation/detection/validation/Save flow before or after initial setup. Missing/invalid files are reported without secrets. | Review intended activation; no replacement tunnel creation. Merely detecting files, launching/status, or entering a menu never changes modes or starts a tunnel. |
| Change token / select A or B later | Select either method after setup; preserve inactive token/config/credential material and validate candidate first. | One committed method; invalid/unfinished candidate leaves prior generation intact. Off stays Off unless explicit On/Start. |

For A use official [dashboard creation guide](https://developers.cloudflare.com/cloudflare-one/networks/connectors/cloudflare-tunnel/get-started/create-remote-tunnel/) with account-neutral link. A asks only runtime token, never installation command/browser cookie/management API key. B fresh default is supported browser login/CLI-create; historical scoped API creation is alternative evidence, not a default extra key prompt or new menu. No account identifier embedded.

The A runtime is equivalent to `cloudflared tunnel run --token <token>`, with CFMgr reading the saved value as data from `/jffs/addons/CFMgr.d/config`. For supported versions, generate a private transient token file and use `--token-file` so the saved secret does not appear in process arguments. This file is a derived runtime handoff, not a second source of truth. Cloudflare documents that capability from `2025.4.0`; prove the supported binary floor and stale/default-YAML isolation in P1. Never silently fall back to exposing a token in arguments. Both methods retain `--no-autoupdate` under the shared updater's control. See [run parameters](https://developers.cloudflare.com/cloudflare-one/networks/connectors/cloudflare-tunnel/configure-tunnels/run-parameters/).

Fresh B creates only a hostless starter; afterward user owns full routing/ingress/origin/arbitrary options/comments. CFMgr owns exactly YAML keys tunnel, credentials-file and origincert (the latter only when a saved certificate path was actually selected), plus private runtime JSON derived from main config. Import/repair/reinstall never regenerates application YAML. Administrative cert.pem differs from runtime JSON; UUID-and-JSON runtime does not require one merely for presence. See [tunnel permissions](https://developers.cloudflare.com/cloudflare-one/networks/connectors/cloudflare-tunnel/do-more-with-tunnels/local-management/tunnel-permissions/) and scoped recovery below.

| Advanced validation / switch step | Required behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Safe local inputs | Verify mounted/writable Entware, supported executable, selected readable YAML, and private permissions. Reject blank/placeholding/malformed configuration; do not execute/source it or introduce a YAML-parser dependency. Use cloudflared's parser plus tested native validation for required values. |
| One tunnel identity and credential set | Require real UUID/matching valid JSON; at B import Save capture authoritative AccountTag/TunnelSecret/TunnelID/ref/expected identity in private main config, then derive owned runtime JSON. Validate paths/used cert without disclosure. Multiple identities need explicit setup selection, never guessed adoption or an auth-repair UUID change. |
| Routing/runtime semantics | Validate the selected YAML using bounded cloudflared native checks; for published applications run `cloudflared tunnel --config <path> ingress validate`, including its final catch-all. Private-network-only configurations use their own supported validation path and must not fail just for lacking published-app ingress. Exact parser-only checks for that path remain P1 evidence. |
| Manager-owned runtime controls | Method/logging/update/PID controls consistent with CFMgr. Reconcile ONLY tunnel/credentials-file/origincert YAML keys and private JSON from validated authority, no application rewrite. Optional origincert only for selected saved cert; detect conflicting other token/config/logging options and explain user edits, no unmanaged output/inactive-method discovery. |
| Reviewed activation | Recheck candidate/readiness under locks. On running tunnel uses one bounded stop/start; never overlap connectors. Persist selected mode/eligible integration after validation with rollback. Preserve saved On/Off unless explicitly changed. |
| Completion / future starts | configured=true only for locally valid completed identity/data setup. Explicit reviewed On may be committed before first bounded auth/runtime attempt; verified throttle/network delay yields On waiting, definitive auth failure yields public Off+credential repair. Fresh B without confirmed UUID remains incomplete/prepared under resumable guard/requested On, never fabricated configured identity. Starts obey readiness/method; process survival not connectivity. |

Cloudflare documents YAML shapes for both published applications and private networks plus its native ingress validator in [configuration-file guidance](https://developers.cloudflare.com/cloudflare-one/networks/connectors/cloudflare-tunnel/do-more-with-tunnels/local-management/configuration-file/). Local syntax/credential-shape validation cannot prove a remote tunnel still exists or its token is authorized; report those runtime outcomes accurately without expanding setup into provider administration.

### Typed credential records and scoped runtime/YAML authority

| Boundary / trigger | Required behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Distinct credential types | A runtime token, B AccountTag/TunnelSecret/TunnelID, optional genuinely selected management API credential and account login cert distinct. Default B requests browser authorization, not API key. Login repairs management authority only; a separate authorized same-UUID fetch can obtain current runtime credentials. Minimal repair never silently changes UUID/mode. If recovery cannot succeed, offer the separately reviewed replacement flow D44, not an endless unchanged-secret prompt. |
| Explicit native sharing | DDNS/IP-Sync may reference one explicitly shared compatible typed credential record with validated operation scope. Repair/update it atomically while retaining all settings; do not infer scope/dependencies from equal token strings or dedupe incompatible credential types. Never print stored secret values. Global revocation versus operation-only permission determines verified dependent impact. |
| Authoritative store / runtime derivation | Main full AccountTag/TunnelID/TunnelSecret/ref/expected identity/captured endpoint metadata; missing JSON safely serializes mode600 with SAME account/UUID/secret. A handoff derives token. Selected cert remains normal private Entware data/ref plus validated original-byte JFFS recovery copy with provenance/account/format/hash/generation; no full YAML duplicate in JFFS. Import captures authority and trusted cert at Save, direct trio/JSON edits unsupported/reconciled not adopted. |
| Bounded drift detection | Manager `u`/`uf`/reinstall, watchdog/start and eligible interactive integrity checks compare safely parsed master authority, the owned YAML trio and full derived JSON with trusted generation, without Scribe dependency or filesystem scans. Healthy matching state never forces restore/overwrite, JFFS resnapshot, restart, download or provider work. User-owned YAML/ingress changes are preserved and are not owned drift or restart triggers. Missing whole YAML requires known-good `.bak`, private transaction copy, manual archive/import or reviewed repair; never fabricate starter YAML or lose ingress. |
| Lossless owned-field reconciliation | Validate parsed authoritative values/matching expected identity first; stage/reinsert missing ONLY tunnel/credentials-file/origincert keys and derived JSON under locks/journal/generation. origincert only if selected saved path configured; API-created B without issued cert gets no bogus path/default certificate dependency. Preserve all other YAML/comments/foreign files, recheck concurrent content/generation before recoverable publication. Ambiguous/unsupported YAML defers, never full serialization/editor/shadow YAML. Native parsing/duplicate-key/lossless edits Pending. |
| Changed runtime material | Only validated selected runtime credential/owned-content change coalesces one readiness-gated INTERNAL restart of eligible verified connector. Preserve activation/method/maintenance/update/integration/foreign processes. No restart from mtime alone, inactive-mode values, management-token-only change, healthy menu checks or irrelevant application edits. Off stays Off; mount/readiness/concurrency failure defers/guards rather than mixed credentials. |
| No usable authority / damaged user YAML | No validated identity/known-good authority means reviewed repair, never guess UUID/credential or create remote replacement. Invalid nonowned YAML remains existing corruption/quarantine path; preserve application input/recovery copies rather than overwrite with template. Confirmed rejected authority is auth_fault/public Off, distinct from benign runtime-file drift. |
| Missing derived data / whole YAML | Missing JSON or owned key with valid trusted main fields and otherwise valid preserved user YAML is repairable derived drift; stage/validate regeneration under guard, one eligible internal restart only when needed, Off never starts. Missing whole user config.yml requires known-good FULL private recovery/archive copy or reviewed import/repair; never fabricate 404 starter as recovered user config. Unknown authority/nonowned YAML damage stays quarantine. |
| Missing selected management certificate | Restore runtime cert only from validated original-byte JFFS copy with matching account/format/provenance/hash/generation; capture new copy only on successful reviewed login/import/repair replacement. A missing copy may be seeded once from a trusted existing runtime cert during explicit schema migration/reinstall after provenance/account validation; never adopt arbitrary edited/foreign certs. Explicit fresh/replacement B creation or accepted existing-UUID credential retrieval may invoke supported browser login only when neither trusted copy is usable; login alone never supplies runtime secret, and copying does not renew revoked/rejected auth. Healthy UUID+JSON runtime/launch/boot/watchdog/import/Re-detect never logs in merely because runtime cert is absent. Backup/restore covers both owned cert copies as one generation; either validated source can rebuild runtime copy. Rejected management permission/account is repaired at that operation, not automatic new-tunnel creation. |
| Login acquisition / provenance | Login issues account management cert; create issues new UUID/JSON, while explicit token --cred-file fetch obtains credentials for an existing UUID. Reuse validated same-account cert or reviewed login/display terminal-only callback URL/bounded cancelable wait. Require current child plus supported format/account/attempt provenance, not exit 0/file appearance; existing-cert refusal may exit 0. --origincert is not assumed login output redirect. Preserve foreign/default cert, no HOME override/headless dependency. Exact installed-version path/provenance/callback/cancel/manual cert import proof Pending P1. |
| Manual main-config edit | Safely parse/validate changed authoritative data/schema/credential/identity through same reconciliation, never source/eval. Already On runtime change may coalesce one internal restart; otherwise Off remains Off until accepted repair/fx/explicit On authorizes validated retry of still-requested faulted work. Intentional Off/shared unrelated work unaffected, auth_fault never bypassed by file presence. |

Reinstall/update and every tunnel start must use the same selected-method integrity gate before activation. For advanced B, compare JSON account/UUID/secret and the owned YAML trio against validated master authority; for A, validate the saved token/expected identity and its owned private handoff instead. An unconfigured feature or selected A method does not require inactive B YAML/JSON, and inactive files never trigger a mode change or restart. Missing Entware defers only storage-dependent reconciliation and tunnel activation; it does not prevent the manager package update or native features. A matching runtime stays untouched. A missing JFFS cert copy may be seeded once from trusted validated source without restoring matching runtime files.

Runtime auth/connectivity evidence and local credential-shape validity are separate. Source-tested failure classification, exact typed record/schema/ref scope, native JSON serialization/lossless YAML edits, bounded fingerprints and internal restart concurrency remain Pending. User application YAML ownership does not extend to CFMgr-owned identity/credential fields.

Certificate validation must follow supported Cloudflared credential format, not assume ordinary X.509 or require openssl x509. Upstream [origin-certificate decoder](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflared/blob/master/credentials/origin_cert.go) reads ARGO TUNNEL TOKEN credential PEM with account/zone/API-token and optional endpoint metadata, tolerating documented legacy blocks. Keep COMPLETE original bytes as recovery source, never parse/reserialize into a fabricated certificate. Source-version/native format/account validation proof remains P1; copying cannot renew a revoked embedded management credential.

### Advanced B creation and import

B creation/import is a supported requirement. Its first setup prompt is `Auto-detect/import existing advanced config? [y/N]`; n/blank is the fresh-creation default. Parse y/n/e completely, reject other input, and honour Ctrl+C. Import exists only for advanced B, while A is complete token-based setup. Entering B may collect/prep inputs but never silently create or overwrite before Save.

| B prompt/step | Required behavior |
| --- | --- |
| y — prepare/detect | Verify Entware, prepare/show `/opt/etc/cloudflared/` and a private incoming `/opt/etc/cloudflared/import/` location if needed. Detect existing candidates read-only; do not replace a live config while the user places files. Show where `config.yml`, matching JSON, and any required certificate belong. |
| y — place files / next prompt | Prompt the user to place files, then press Enter to continue, or e/Ctrl+C to cancel before Save. Re-scan after this prompt; selecting y itself is not proof files exist. If several candidates exist, select one explicitly. Missing files return to the placement step without marking setup complete. |
| y — validate/import | Require supported YAML syntax, actual UUID, matching valid JSON, and every referenced file needed by the chosen config. Preserve user application options; any necessary owned-path remapping is shown explicitly. Review Save/enable, commit the selected advanced generation, then manage/start according to the activation choice. |
| n or blank — fresh setup | Collect minimum tunnel name/intended account context/overwrite review. Reviewed Save authorizes checked installer, reuse of validated same-account cert or guarded login if absent, then exactly selected CLI named create/private JSON/hostless starter. Login displays browser authorization URL and waits for current-attempt success/validated certificate. No management API token default prompt, headless browser/device-code requirement or automatic app/DNS/policy setup. |
| Existing files found on fresh path | Warn before Save and explain y-import/e alternatives. Ordinary fully fresh setup may create a hostless starter after reviewed overwrite. Credential-recovery replacement instead carries forward validated FULL application YAML/comments/ingress while changing only owned identity/path fields; make private inventoried config.yml.bak before publication. If safe carry-forward is impossible, preserve original/.bak and explain manual re-patching rather than silently dropping ingress. Preserve old trusted generation/foreign files until coherent commit, stop old owned connector before one new runtime; tell user to manually delete old remote tunnel, never delete it automatically. |
| Completed setup | Apply the common native validation, one-tunnel mode switch, reviewed enable, maintenance, and Scribe gates. A hostless valid starter is allowed. Later re-detect imports advanced config only; normal repair/reinstall preserves user edits. |

| Authentication route | Supported behavior / minimum inputs | Planning status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Native cloudflared CLI — fresh B default | After reviewed Save reuse validated same-account cert, or if absent launch cloudflared tunnel login, show ephemeral URL only in terminal, intentionally wait/cancel and verify current-attempt cert/account/format. Then cloudflared tunnel create selected name; capture full runtime authority/UUID, preserve old/foreign files until coherent replacement. | Selected default; native child/trap/write-path/default-cert/no-overwrite/account/CLI-create/unknown-outcome proof Pending P1. No API-key/browser dependency; exit 0/file presence alone not current-attempt success. No login/create on ordinary launch or import/re-detect. |
| Scoped management API creation — historical alternative evidence | Previously reviewed API account/name/scoped-token/local-mode route constructs runtime JSON without issuing cert. | Earlier API-first default superseded by D42. No new API submenu/wizard requirement; retain optional typed key only if genuinely selected alternative. Source/API research remains evidence, not default code plan. |
| B-y import | User places existing completed YAML and required JSON/certificate files, then continues the import prompt flow. | Fully supported advanced-only import branch; no new remote tunnel or administration credentials required. |

The API-creation alternative is reference evidence, not an additional executable route, wizard or credential requirement for the initial implementation. Existing API-created locally managed tunnels use the ordinary B-y runtime-file import; no API management key is required to run their UUID/JSON.

The official [local-tunnel workflow](https://developers.cloudflare.com/cloudflare-one/networks/connectors/cloudflare-tunnel/do-more-with-tunnels/local-management/create-local-tunnel/) documents login, named creation, and generated credentials as separate commands. The [login implementation](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflared/blob/master/cmd/cloudflared/tunnel/login.go) downloads an account certificate after browser authorization; its [transfer helper](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflared/blob/master/token/transfer.go) prints the authorization URL regardless of browser-open success and performs bounded transfer polling. Installed-version limits/path behavior remain P1 proof. The [creation implementation](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflared/blob/master/cmd/cloudflared/tunnel/subcommand_context.go) reads the account certificate for CLI administration and writes tunnel JSON. A normal scoped API token uses the separate [Tunnel creation API](https://developers.cloudflare.com/api/resources/zero_trust/subresources/tunnels/subresources/cloudflared/methods/create/), not the connector-token argument. No supported API-token-to-account-certificate flow is established by these sources.

The [official runtime credential struct](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflared/blob/master/connection/connection.go) establishes AccountTag/TunnelSecret/TunnelID; capture full validated fields/supported metadata from CLI-created or imported JSON as main authority. Optional genuinely selected API management credential remains distinct; default B asks none. Runtime needs no repeated management login/cert merely to run UUID+JSON; missing cert repair is for actually dependent management only.

Fresh B collects minimum name/intended account context and Save review, reuses validated same-account certificate or displays login URL/waits only if absent; never asks password/cookies/API key as substitute. Cert and creation output stay private staged/Entware until validated generation; the JFFS copy is the validated original bytes, not a duplicate write on each setup. Actual login paths/default existing-cert behavior/current-child/provenance/manual downloaded-cert fallback must be proven. Minimal later repair asks only actual failed credential/cert; explicit supported relogin alone never creates replacement tunnel. Missing certificate alone on an existing installation is no login trigger.

Validate name/account/permissions and stage private local inputs before Save; remote creation is a save-transaction mutation. Confirm new UUID and record provenance immediately; handle duplicate names, retries, local failure, cancellation, and response loss without blindly creating another tunnel. Warn/recoverably replace selected old local YAML/credential files only at fresh Save; never delete/adopt pre-existing remote resources as failure cleanup. Keep the old valid generation until replacement begins; incomplete/damaged commits are quarantined instead of silently resumed. Recovery is confined to verified transaction/resource identity.

After reviewed Save, record a small durable creation-in-progress intent BEFORE launching the owned CLI child: selected account/name/generation/output-path provenance, separate from active authority. Let the supported CLI generate the secret and capture its COMPLETE validated JSON into main authority only after a confirmed result; no mandatory pre-generated secret, extra RNG dependency or TUNNEL_CREATE_SECRET handoff. Use a fresh private credentials output path, never overwrite foreign files or expose credentials. Interruption/nonzero exit is not evidence that no remote tunnel was created.

Treat remote creation, JSON writing, authoritative JFFS promotion and activation separately. Recovery is limited to a confirmed existing UUID/account plus complete validated credentials from the master config or trusted current-attempt staged result; repair local persistence with that SAME identity, never create again. Preserve the only recoverable staged data/old generation on write failure and report path/permission errors; incomplete data is not configured/On-ready. The [upstream creation source](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflared/blob/master/cmd/cloudflared/tunnel/subcommand_context.go) attempts to delete its newly created tunnel if JSON writing fails, but CFMgr adds no remote deletion/discovery: nonzero exit or local rollback cannot prove absence, and known deleted resources never activate.

If NEW creation fails, remains incomplete or has an unconfirmed result, keep an inactive creation-failed/unknown marker and report any confirmed UUID without secrets. Prompt the user to check Zero Trust and manually delete any failed/partially created tunnel before another explicitly reviewed creation attempt. No automatic account listing, guessed-resource adoption, unknown-result reconciliation, remote deletion or background create retry. Known ID alone cannot reconstruct a secret. Auth/permission/write errors explain what must be fixed; missing/vanished mount hard-stops Cloudflared work until the expected drive returns. Previously valid settings/foreign resources remain recoverable and unrelated features remain usable. This D43 policy supersedes automatic unknown-create reconciliation and pre-generated-secret requirements.

| Creation failure / storage observation | Required response |
| --- | --- |
| Confirmed UUID and complete trusted runtime credentials, local save failed | Guard activation, preserve current-attempt data, explain write/permission error and allow explicit same-identity local recovery once storage writable. Validate runtime auth separately; no second create. |
| Missing JSON for an already configured known tunnel | Regenerate from validated main AccountTag/TunnelID/TunnelSecret with matching UUID; do not clear an existing auth fault or loop rebuilding known-rejected unchanged values. |
| Failed/incomplete create, only ID or unknown remote result | Warn that a failed tunnel may exist, show known ID if any and direct manual Zero Trust inspection/deletion. Leave attempted setup inactive; only explicit reviewed fresh retry after cleanup acknowledgement, no automatic reconciliation/create. |
| JSON/config write or management auth/permission error | Report relevant file/operation without secrets; preserve trusted recovery and return to the narrow correction flow or menu. Do not start with incomplete or rejected credentials; definitive auth retains scoped Off/fx behavior. |
| Expected Entware mount disappears at any Cloudflared stage | Stop/reap owned child boundedly, gate tunnel/update/repair writes, report unavailable drive and possible failed creation if request may have been sent. Return menu without waiting; retry only after same expected mounted writable volume verified. |
| Mount later returns / normal hooks fire | Resume only eligible previously configured On/maintenance work through normal guards/readiness. Failed/unknown creation never resumes itself; native recovery/status/Off remain available; operational features enforce their shared prerequisites. |


The generated YAML may contain ordinary `#` comments and examples; published-app rules still require a final catch-all. A proposed valid starter has no active hostname/origin mapping and returns only 404:

```yaml
tunnel: <TUNNEL-UUID>
credentials-file: /opt/etc/cloudflared/credentials/<TUNNEL-UUID>.json
ingress:
  # Add your own application rules above the final catch-all, for example:
  # - hostname: app.example.com
  #   service: http://127.0.0.1:8080
  - service: http_status:404
```

Replace UUID/path only after confirmed creation; examples stay comments, no DNS/network/policy changes. Hostless starter can complete local setup with reviewed On/readiness. User owns subsequent application YAML; CFMgr owns identity/credential link and derived JSON per scoped authority. Repair/reinstall/switch preserves user content, later explicit fresh B separately warns/replaces at Save. Advanced examples belong in guides.

### Dedicated feature setup sequence

Base installation finishes before any of these flows. Shared internal helpers may implement validation/storage/transactions, but DDNS, Cloudflared, and IP-Sync each have a distinct submenu-driven setup/install path.

1. Open the selected Setup and run scope-specific read-only checks, preserving other features. DDNS/IP-Sync require native firmware/JFFS/RAM only; Cloudflared A/B and standalone maintenance additionally require mounted Entware. The common Cloudflared installer runs at Save; standalone skips tunnel setup.
2. Collect only chosen inputs and mask secrets. DDNS/IP-Sync checkpoint each completed admissible field to the private resumable draft: token/account/zone/list progress survives e/Ctrl+C. A/B use private staging before reviewed Save; B asks y/N import versus fresh and prepares incoming paths. No draft changes active config/credentials or marks missing setup complete.
3. Once required values/files validate, optionally detect mounted Entware plus usable Scribe/logrotate and stage first-setup logging only when ready. Without them native DDNS/IP-Sync setup continues with no custom files; native messages remain. Preserve saved preferences/off choices; Cloudflared starts size-based and offers later tuning, DDNS/IP-Sync fixed defaults. Backup setup remains separate/optional.
4. Validate input with bounded read-only checks where possible. Resolve account/zone/list ambiguity using the feature's flow, not guesses or another feature's credentials.
5. Review selected resources/files/dependencies/hooks/activation and fresh-B overwrite warning; require explicit Save for active promotion. Before it, DDNS/IP-Sync e/Ctrl+C retain draft progress, while Cloudflared discards only transient uncommitted inputs. Preserve other active features and tunnel applications/routes.
6. Save writes guard/recovery then authorizes checked binary installer and selected fresh/replacement B CLI create: reuse validated same-account cert, login/showURL/current-attempt validation only if absent. Intentional interactive browser wait safely cancellable; e/Back refused during active commit, Ctrl+C honored with honest incomplete/recovery state/owned child cleanup. Only native DDNS/IP-Sync private draft checkpointing is a pre-Save persistence exception; it authorizes no active/provider mutation. Old active files remain recoverable until coherent replacement; stop old owned connector before activating selected new identity, record new resource provenance, never delete prior remote tunnel. User archives manual.
7. Publish only locally completed validated settings/identity/generation and reviewed On, then eligible hooks/start/first API attempt through guards. A runtime-valid setup may be On waiting-throttle/network; definitive auth failure records fault then public Off/unhooks and enters failed-credential-only repair. Incomplete fresh B without confirmed UUID retains prepared/requested On under resumable guard, never configured=true; unknown create outcome never repeats blindly. Scribe only completed setup, no fabricated setup from credentials.
8. Report truthful complete/Off-auth/waiting/incomplete result and return or offer bounded credential-only flow. Repeated confirmed auth rejection returns to that prompt; e/Ctrl+C leaves safe Off/fault. Rate/network returns menu/deferred work, no token prompt. Pre-Save failure of staged credential leaves prior valid generation intact; post-Save interruption uses rollback/quarantine or specifically recorded resumable outcome, never guessed activation.

### Native setup drafts and retry loops

DDNS and IP-Sync must retain completed setup inputs on early exit. After each complete syntactically admissible field, write a bounded private atomic draft checkpoint on JFFS: token/API credential, account ID where applicable, DDNS zone/record progress or IP-Sync target/family progress. Mask secrets and label API validation separately as unchecked/accepted/rejected; saved text is not verified authority. Never save a partially typed field interrupted by Ctrl+C. Checkpoint on accepted input/stage changes, not keystrokes or background ticks. A disk/permission/rename failure reports that progress was not saved, preserves the last good draft and cannot silently claim resumability.

Drafts are separate from active configuration and shared credential references. First-time incomplete setup remains Configured:false/Enabled:false with no operational jobs or writes. An incomplete replacement or second-list draft leaves the existing committed configuration/primary target operating under its saved state. Changing draft account/token/zone invalidates dependent validation without losing useful typed input; revalidate target identity before final Save. Draft persistence cannot clear an auth fault or enable intentionally Off work. Atomic final Save merges into the latest valid config and overwrites its explicitly accepted fields even if manually edited meanwhile, preserving unrelated fields/guards, then retires only that draft; interrupted promotion uses the ordinary guard/recovery rules.

Bare e in DDNS/IP-Sync input exits the whole Setup to its feature menu after retaining completed progress; Ctrl+C exits the script safely after bounded owned-child cleanup. Next Setup automatically loads the draft, indicates which values are already saved without revealing credentials, and resumes the first missing/invalid step with an option to edit saved fields. Interactive manager launch may show a local setup-incomplete/resume notice, but makes no discovery/API request unless the user enters/resumes Setup. Ordinary incomplete drafts do not invoke corruption containment or stop an unrelated/previously valid service.

An accepted wizard Save is the latest explicit authority for exactly the fields shown/selected in that setup, including an explicitly reviewed On/Off choice. Under a short lock, reload safely parsed current config, preserve unrelated values and active removal/reset/auth guards, overlay the accepted fields, then publish a new generation. Concurrent manual edits to those fields are overwritten without another conflict prompt or API permission-to-overwrite check. A later Off or valid manual config edit becomes the next authority; no stale worker/wizard may replay its old generation. Runtime detects relevant content changes even when a manual editor did not increment revision. Invalid schema/config still needs recovery, and incomplete drafts never overwrite active/shared credentials.

For primary or second IP-list exact-name lookup, a complete successful lookup with zero exact matches prints **List not found** and immediately repeats **Enter the exact list name (e to exit):**. There is no separate retry confirmation, return to the menu or token/account prompt. Repeat until a valid exact name is selected or bare e/Ctrl+C exits; numbers in this retry loop are literal names, not picker indexes. Preserve the original draft credentials and retry only the name/lookup step. Incomplete enumeration, auth/permission failure, throttle or network error has its own message and bounded retry/cooldown handling; none is misreported as List not found. Quoted 'e' or "e" is an explicit literal-name candidate, while bare e is navigation; optional/literal quote ambiguity still follows the name-input contract. DDNS uses the same retained-draft/resume behavior for incomplete zone/record selection, with precise lookup failures and no guessed target.

### Cancellation and save points

Use shared POSIX signal/ownership helpers with feature-specific persistence. Reviewed Save begins an active durable transaction, not merely a nonempty field. DDNS/IP-Sync private draft checkpoints and prepared import directories are allowed before Save; active values, provider writes, installs, hook edits and service transitions are not. Read-only discovery occurs only in explicitly entered setup/check flows. Cancellation cannot undo a remote resource already created after Save.

| Stage/input | Required behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Native DDNS/IP-Sync input, e | Save/retain completed private draft fields and exit Setup to the feature menu. Do not step back through credential prompts or replace active settings. Existing valid config remains intact; first incomplete setup stays inactive. |
| Native DDNS/IP-Sync input, Ctrl+C / EOF / termination | Bound cleanup of this wizard's children/transient secrets, restore terminal echo, retain last durable completed fields and exit. Never promote a partial field/generation or stop healthy unrelated work. EOF is not consent; abrupt kill/power loss retains at least the last successful checkpoint. |
| Cloudflared input before reviewed Save, e / Ctrl+C | e cancels/returns; Ctrl+C exits safely, discarding transient uncommitted input while preserving existing credentials/generation and user-placed import files. A healthy detached connector is not killed merely because its menu exits. |
| Explicit Save accepted | Persist affected-scope intent/recovery before any active mutation; block mixed generations. For Cloudflared, announce e unavailable from this point through installation/login/create/config publication/activation until success, verified rollback or guarded failure. Preserve newly created identity/credentials recoverably before subsequent publication. |
| Cloudflared mutation, e | Refuse Back with save/setup-in-progress notice; typed e cannot abandon or reset the transaction. During login it does not become a browser-cancel command. Ctrl+C remains available; do not ignore signals or unboundedly lock the user into setup. |
| Active commit, Ctrl+C / termination | Interrupt/reap only owned children, do bounded cleanup and release locks. Defer only a tiny atomic marker/rename boundary. If completion/rollback is unproved, leave explicit incomplete/repair-required guard, preserve candidate/recovery data, contain affected owned connector/jobs and exit nonzero. Do not intentionally damage valid data or claim interruption always corrupts it. |
| Native draft checkpoint / final promotion | e remains available throughout normal native prompts. A short atomic checkpoint may complete before returning; it is not a Cloudflared-style whole-wizard navigation lock. Final active promotion remains a guarded atomic transaction; signals cannot expose half-written active state. |
| After completed Save / normal menus | e and Ctrl+C exit without undoing valid settings. Ordinary submenus retain Back behavior. Later damage still invokes the health gate. Bare e is navigation; explicitly quoted literal names are handled by the dedicated picker. |
| Killed process / power loss | Traps are not guaranteed. Durable native checkpoints preserve completed progress; active-mutation journals/generations catch interruption. Cloudflared next setup/launch enters recovery first and cannot blindly repeat unknown remote creation. |

Verify traps/child descriptor lifetime/terminal echo and bounded cleanup on native BusyBox. Never delete foreign state, print secrets or save a partial input buffer. Draft schema/permissions/revision checks and active journal ordering are P1/P2 evidence work.

### Startup integrity and setup recovery

At interactive manager launch, perform a bounded local health check before normal menus or Scribe/hook self-healing: safely parse the main config/schema/state, inspect the independently readable setup journal/committed generation, and validate required selected runtime files when present/changed. Use cached fingerprints plus bounded native parser checks where applicable; no network/API/update check. A newly installed manager with all features unconfigured/off is healthy. Native resumable drafts are inert and trigger a local resume notice, not quarantine. Interrupted Cloudflared mutation is distinct: automatically enter local recovery detection/containment before normal setup/menu work; prompt reviewed recovery before any new Cloudflared action. Expected unavailable Entware is storage deferral, not evidence that saved config should be reset.

| Detected condition | Required response |
| --- | --- |
| Healthy committed generation | Bounded state/fingerprint checks, active-flag hook/Scribe reconciliation; no unconditional restart/download/provider call. Valid changed selected runtime authority may request one scoped internal readiness restart, unchanged fast path untouched. Persistent auth faults trigger local repair prompt/fx warning, never blind auto-On or fresh auth poll. Missing intentionally uninstalled binary remains installation state. |
| Repairable derived JSON / owned YAML trio drift | Trusted matching full main credential/expected identity + valid user YAML permits journaled scoped rebuild/reinsert of JSON/tunnel/credentials-file/selected origincert only; Off never starts, eligible changed runtime one internal readiness restart. Whole missing user YAML needs known-good full data recovery/import, never template. No authority/nonowned damage remains repair-required. |
| Interrupted commit / unrecoverable authority or user YAML / invalid activation | Set affected repair-required gate before suppressing starts/jobs; verified owned process bounded containment uses independent records, never foreign process/name kill. Preserve data/recovery, suspend affected runtime/updater/watchdog/boot/WAN/mount until reviewed valid generation; benign derived drift uses row above, confirmed auth instead fault/public Off. |
| Main CFMgr config malformed / cannot safely establish feature state | Preserve the file, known-good recovery material, and all credentials/options. Quarantine CFMgr-managed activation; stop verified owned tunnel instances and suppress all affected CFMgr-owned feature/maintenance blocks/jobs until main configuration is repaired. Never infer safe enabled flags from malformed text or silently initialize fresh defaults. |
| Cleanup / process termination | Set the health gate first so concurrent hooks/watchdog cannot respawn. Use bounded TERM/wait and recheck PID/executable/ownership before any signal; a verified owned process still alive after the deadline may require targeted KILL during corruption containment, never blanket killall. If ownership/cleanup is uncertain, keep quarantine and report it. Preserve other controllers, hook content, and cron entries. Healthy menu startup stays quick; exceptional cleanup reports its bounded wait. |
| Next interactive launch or Setup / unresolved Cloudflared recovery | Initiate bounded local recovery assessment/containment before normal setup/menu actions. Explain interruption and retained state, then open the recovery entry with Repair incomplete/damaged setup now? [y/N]. y proceeds through reviewed same-identity/known-good/import options. Decline/e/Ctrl+C may leave affected work guarded/inactive while unrelated native work remains usable; it never dismisses the unresolved marker. No auto login/fresh B create, unknown-result lookup, remote deletion or overwrite. Repeat recovery entry on later launch until resolved. |
| Noninteractive boot/WAN/cron handler | Check the same health marker/state locally; never start, reinstall missing hooks, update binaries, or make affected provider calls while quarantined. No prompt; emit a brief bounded redacted notice and return/defer. |
| Successful reviewed recovery | Validate complete coherent generation, reconcile only eligible owned blocks/jobs, preserve unrelated values and saved Off choices, then clear quarantine durably. Only eligible enabled ready work resumes. Corruption discovery never deletes remote resources/creates a tunnel. Unresolved uninstall/reset intent requires cleanup recovery rather than setup reconstruction. |

The containment rule is a separate exception to normal healthy hook/lifecycle behavior, not a request to reset preferences. A tunnel-file problem isolates Cloudflared while valid DDNS/IP-Sync can remain active; a broken shared main config cannot safely authorize either feature. Existing Scribe rotation/history can be retained, but logging activation/automatic rule repair must not bless an incomplete setup. Root `0` status remains observational after launch safety handling; standalone CLI status reports invalid/recovery state read-only. No unseen global hook replacement or foreign-service stop is justified by corruption in CFMgr.

### DDNS and IP-Sync hook state and self-healing

Derive desired operational hooks/cron from valid saved configured/enabled state and the recovery gate, not from credential presence or leftover files. Keep feature activation separate from custom logging preference. Hook reconciliation never clears API keys, zone/account/list/record IDs, source/schedule settings, or configured state just because integrations are removed.
